# 2.3 困ったときは… | 日本語 | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/3.md)
.

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/3#id-1)

2.3.1 曲の音が割れていたりして使えない!！


------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/3#id-1-1)

2.3.1.1 バッファサイズ

[(see 2.2.1.1)](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#1-1)
 Your buffer size may be too small, resulting in buffer underruns. To fix this, increase your buffer size. Note that this will increase latency.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/3#id-1-2)

2.3.1.2 オーディオインターフェース

[(see 2.2.4)](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#4)
 Certain audio interface configurations don't play nice with LMMS. If you are on macOS or Windows, make sure you have SDL selected.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/3#id-1-3)

2.3.1.3 トラックの音量

Your audio within LMMS may be too loud, resulting in digital clipping. This can be fixed by reducing the volume of the instruments in LMMS (either with the "VOL" knobs or through the mixer).

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/3#id-232-mokoenai)

2.3.2 何も聞こえない！


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/3#id-2321-diointfsu)

2.3.2.1 オーディオインターフェース

[(see 2.2.4)](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#4)
 Certain audio interface configurations (specifically Dummy) just don't work sometimes. If you are on macOS or Windows, make sure you have SDL selected.

# 钢琴卷帘窗（Piano-Roll） | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll.md)
.

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FVmBP3jbyXsSymRtRnbR6%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D97ecf994-2ed3-40cb-9aa0-5a1c85163d40&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7435277a&sv=2)

钢琴卷帘窗

钢琴卷帘窗是处理旋律与和弦的主要场所。它可以通过对下列对象进行双击或右键唤出上下文菜单打开：

*   一个乐器轨道的片段，像这样：![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FExMpAn47fnp77l87S15Z%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D18ae6aa6-e675-438a-bb9f-018940e65269&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5362f3a6&sv=2) 。
    
*   样式编辑器中一个轨道的步进。
    

您也可以通过使用工具栏中的 **显示/隐藏钢琴窗** ![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FpZYoJDxFjfm60HPLP9UU%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dbf3db9aa-fc16-4d72-8432-c2bcef50d287&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3ce3ad53&sv=2) 或快捷键 F7（在 1.3 版本中为 Ctrl+3 ）。

请注意，**显示/隐藏钢琴窗** 按钮打开的是最后编辑的片段。如果您刚刚开始一个新的会话 (不要与开始一个新项目混淆)，您必须首先将钢琴窗连接到一个片段才能使用它。LMMS 不会记住从一个会话到另一个会话的最后编辑片段。

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll#gong-ju-lan)

工具栏


---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FVuW12tphI8FobAcujt2u%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Def5c9089-1161-46e2-8fcb-e48af3259c3b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=76840ace&sv=2)

钢琴卷帘窗的顶部工具栏

很多的工具和歌曲编辑器中的相类似，下面是一些值得注意的稍微不一样的地方：

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll#bo-fang-he-lu-zhi-playback-and-record)

播放和录制（Playback and Record）

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FHAk8oFeNbRhh4WDEWy6G%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da930e9c6-9cd4-40c1-9661-70645395eb76&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c0cbbaf6&sv=2)

播放和录制按钮

除了播放和停止控件之外，还加了两个用于 **录制** 的按钮用于录制以下两种音符输入：

*   附带的 MIDI 键盘
    
*   您的计算机键盘
    

点击 ![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FIG4qsJPjJi1KmwBRmy55%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D27fa7780-db52-40bd-a7cd-50bc264aa87e&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=efce19ad&sv=2) 可以在不播放歌曲编辑器/样式编辑器的环境下录制，![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FEZI9ohlgZmbygJmDKRgn%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc47c43f0-6bce-48df-a93b-82e4ded54c4d&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2f965d14&sv=2) 则可以在播放歌曲编辑器/样式编辑器的环境下录制。 点击 ![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FOZenYsvUlahBQAqcahJP%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9e29b4c6-9667-4afb-b9f9-dd2ca610e7c3&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c957a71b&sv=2) 可以停止播放和录制。键盘上的 **空格键** 可以执行播放/停止（播放和录制）。 从 1.3 开始, ![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252F3DkbQD1sEWvQx2x4U69g%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D38476df0-bbfc-42a2-ba0d-cbe6f9928fe0&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=864452cd&sv=2) 允许您一次录制一个音符。

当您按下停止，您可以在钢琴窗看到所有播放的音符。这些音符和别的音符一样可以被编辑。

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll#bian-ji-editing)

编辑（Editing）

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FXMBwHIGkZKfQp8KgErZ3%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc5679b87-721e-4db1-a673-9f86cd7325db&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ec8929a0&sv=2)

编辑工具

被选择的音符可以被统一处理。下面是一些快捷键：

*   移动被选音符：鼠标左键拖拽 。
    
*   复制被选音符：Shift + 鼠标左键拖拽 。
    
*   定量移动被选音符（ Shift / Ctrl + 方向键 ）。Shift 会每次移动被选音符一拍（竖方向就是一个半音），而 Ctrl 会每次移动被选音符一个小节（竖方向就是一个八度）。
    

四个编辑模式（工具）分别是 **绘制** 、**擦除** 、**选择** 和 **变调** 。 在钢琴窗的网格中，您也可以通过左键 / 右键来 **绘制** / **移除** 音符。（本章的“编辑注释”中有更详细的解释）。

**选择** 模式工具允许您自由选择音符，使之组成一组“被选择的”音符。您可以对它们进行统一操作。

您可以在选择模式下做出以下操作，被选择的音符会被渲染为蓝色：

*   **框选：**用鼠标拉出一个矩形框，确认选区后松开鼠标，被矩形框触及到的音符会被选择。
    
*   **单选：**直接左键单击来选择一个音符。
    
*   **逐个多选：**您可以通过按住 Shift 键来逐个点击音符，被点击的音符会被选择。如果您 Shift + 左键拖拽出了一个矩形选框，那么其中被选择的音符会反而取消选择，而未被选择的音符会被选择（即反选）。
    
*   **取消选择全部音符：**直接左键单击钢琴窗网格中的一个空白区域即可。
    

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FBWLk1Ywmlx6QpAHy75Nj%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D50256851-97bd-48ce-927b-700f7dd32bc0&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e0c356d9&sv=2)

音符变调的自动化控制 （注意与自动化编辑器相区分）

**变调** 模式允许您编辑单个音符的音高变化。在该模式下选中一个音符，将会出现对应该音符的一个自动化控制编辑器。接着，您就可以在这里面通过鼠标来描点音高的变化曲线，这样音符就会在播放时按您绘制的音高曲线移调。 留意被您移调过的音符，在钢琴中会显示去对应的音高曲线。

移调工具对 VST 乐器无效。您可能还是要在乐器窗口中 VST 自带的 PITCH（音高）旋钮来改变音符音高。

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll#jian-tie-ban-clipboard)

剪贴板（Clipboard）

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FqBAKsdHDWcxdEe15l1qk%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db8b077fc-b7fc-4afd-887c-abbac038a3ab&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=290f630a&sv=2)

剪贴板工具

剪贴板工具允许您剪切、复制、粘贴被选音符。另外，对被选音符按下 Delete 可以删除它们。

*   按下 **复制** 按钮（或 Ctrl+C ）可以复制被选音符（把它们放到剪贴板里）。按住 Shift 拖拽被选音符到远离原位置的另一个位置，可以复制被选音符到新位置。
    

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll#bo-fang-kong-zhi-playback-control)

播放控制（Playback Control）

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FHnw03CaG9ZpxAXJ89F7R%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da50bb15f-a3f8-4c87-91b1-7f3619a24dc6&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d2eb84e0&sv=2)

播放控制工具

These work the same as the playback controls in the Song Editor. To repatriate loops:

*   **Set right loop-point:** use right mouse button only
    
*   **Set left loop-point:** use right mouse button + Shift
    
*   **(1.3 and later) Set both right/left loop-points**: use right mouse button near desired loop-point
    

Hold Ctrl to place loop-points freely.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll#suo-fang-zoom)

缩放（Zoom）

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FdlEQN4FXrNbw7WnOOA9N%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc0f80663-81b3-4ecc-89d2-9fea4b08607e&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f68ba360&sv=2)

视图缩放工具

该控件的作用和歌曲编辑器中的那个一样，点出下拉菜单可以设置横向缩放。

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FUfEbdXfdF9pR1DDQe6W8%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7d9e932f-14db-4f45-9d46-5768abd107b5&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=de214f29&sv=2)

纵向缩放工具

到了 1.3，支持在歌曲编辑器进行纵向缩放。

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll#liang-hua-quantification)

量化（Quantification）

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252F4FRvEV56LE5TRSA4bymU%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Daac1a914-695c-474e-89fa-2c0102228f82&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3e038fcd&sv=2)

量化工具

该控件决定了量化精度，可以通过下拉菜单进行设置。量化精度允许您在绘制和录制时，以该小节分数作为最小精度来操控音符长度。

例如，将量化设置为 1/1，那么您将只能在整小节的起始位置放置音符，并且只能以 1 小节为单位来调整它们的长度。

如果将量化设置为 1/16，那么您将可以在 1/16 小节的时间间隔上放置音符，并且以 1/16 小节的步进来调整它们的长度。

其他依此类推。

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252F6KADz9diUDsk070D68IG%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9005e80e-a355-49ac-8348-412bafb6acf6&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3f9c8260&sv=2) 将钢琴窗中的音符吸附到离网格最近（这取决于量化精度）的位置。

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll#gong-ju)

工具

1.3 之后，工具控件将在 ![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252Fl99D7TIpyznFx1wZXU0k%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D5db8c7fb-c1d9-4dfb-9ea6-358d710c4765&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=fc533afd&sv=2) 按钮后以下拉菜单的形式出现。

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FAwuVxgjwZDxYp727vRae%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da4453f52-de0e-4d7b-a976-a5061fa2e9f6&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=38eba708&sv=2)

工具

*   **合并（** Shift+G **）：**如果两个音符彼此相邻且音高相同，则将其合并。
    

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FiSTjjfZY1nMWhc4YxJEx%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D2d77d4a2-9628-4ba0-a3cb-cb8c8e6b19d6&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=104383fa&sv=2)

胶水工具

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll#bian-ji-yin-fu)

编辑音符

#### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll#hui-zhi-he-yi-dong-yin-fu)

绘制和移动音符

使用绘制模式工具来放置音符。当您将鼠标悬停在钢琴窗的网格（即钢琴窗的工作区）中时，一条灰色的水平线会跟踪鼠标光标的垂直位置，以帮助您顺回钢琴键来定位当前悬停在哪个音高。

*   左键单击一个网格可以放置一个音符，如果您选择了一个和弦类型，则：
    
    *   以当前音高为根音放置和弦的音符。
        
    *   Shift + 左键单击以琶音模式放置音符（this will only duplicate notes onto themselves if no chord is selected）。
        
    
*   将鼠标光标悬停在音符上，光标会变成十字四向箭头，此时：
    
    *   **直接拖拽** 音符可以改变其时间位置和音高。音符在拖拽过程中会自动吸附到设置好的量化精度的网格上。
        
    *   **Alt + 拖拽** 也可以改变其时间位置和音高，但不会吸附到设置好的量化精度的网格上，而是允许您以 1/192 的时间精度（这是 LMMS 中最大的细分数）进行移动。
        
    
*   将鼠标光标悬停在音符的尾部（右侧），光标会变成水平双向箭头，此时：
    
    *   **直接拖拽** 音符的尾部可以改变其音符长度（可以认为是时值），音符的尾部在拖拽过程中会自动吸附到设置好的量化精度的网格上。
        
    *   **Alt + 拖拽** 也可以改变音符的长度，但不会吸附到设置好的量化精度的网格上，而是允许您以 1/192 的时间精度（这是 LMMS 中最大的细分数）“自由”拖拽。
        
    

在绘制模式下按住 Ctrl 键会将鼠标光标切换为选择模式工具，并在释放时返回绘制模式。

#### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll#ca-chu-yin-fu)

擦除音符

有三个办法移除音符：

*   选择 **擦除模式** 来删除要删除的音符（无论鼠标左键还是右键）。
    
*   在 **绘制模式** 下鼠标右键来删除音符。
    
*   选择 **选择模式**（或 Ctrl+鼠标拖拽 ）选择一至多个音符，然后按 Delete 键。
    

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll#mo-ren-yin-fu-chang-du-note-length)

默认音符长度（Note Length）

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FBuLEMQpUjVjnvG5pzAbt%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D02e4365d-85e3-4102-9fb1-62153f2b28e5&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=35eee28a&sv=2)

音符长度工具

您可以通过下拉菜单来设置 **默认音符长度**（以小节为单位），这个设置决定了当您在钢琴窗中使用绘制模式工具创建音符时，音符的默认长度。 你可以选择一个固定不变的音符长度，也可以选择让下一个音符与上一次绘制的音符长度相同。一旦你已经确定了一个理想的音符长度和位置，通常就会更倾向于使用“上一个音符”这个设置。

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll#yin-fu-li-du-volume-he-sheng-xiang-panning)

音符力度（Volume）和声像（Panning）


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FWgsKx2Rb3l6wCkvg2z7T%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ddbf22795-011b-4059-926b-b78e0cf7cecb&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3e8ed575&sv=2)

单个音符力度

点击钢琴键盘下方的 **Note Volume / Note Panning（音符音量 / 音符声像）** 按钮，可以在音符音量编辑器和音符声像编辑器之间切换。 在音符音量编辑器中，力度（Velocity）会显示为音符下方的一根绿色竖条。 您可以直接在竖条上点击，或者拖动竖条的头部，来改变该音符的力度大小。力度越大，音符自身的颜色越亮；力度越小，音符自身的颜色越暗。

如果您正在使用支持力度感压的 MIDI 键盘，则每个音符的力度会基于 **录制** 自动设定。 音符的力度可能不只影响音量，这由演奏乐器决定。

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FTkgNzAS0EEUxMiXaxKfG%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0f9cc592-26fb-49dd-9c8a-f5c6889e8c4b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3497f896&sv=2)

单个音符声相

**声像（Pan）**表示该音符的音量在右声道与左声道之间的分配比例。 在音符声像编辑器中，音符的声像值显示为绿色竖条。 您可以直接在竖条上点击，或者拖动竖条的头部，来改变该音符的声像的方向和它的强度。基于调配的声像方向和强度，音符自身的一侧颜色会变暗（右：底部变暗，左：顶部变暗）。

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/shu-xi-lmms/gang-qin-juan-lian-chuang-pianoroll#gang-qin-jian)

钢琴键


-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252F79z1f2Ux6PsiLPgeNsYq%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D5b4202ce-6fc7-4e0e-9b17-8bd68985f144&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=99712e42&sv=2)

竖排钢琴键

您可以使用窗口右侧的滚动条。或者直接滑动鼠标滚轮来垂直滚动竖排钢琴键（按住 Shift 再拖拽则可以横向滚动）。

左键单击钢琴键或已绘制的音符，乐器都会演奏相应的音。

您仍可以在钢琴窗内使用电脑键盘来演奏乐器。

您可以通过 设置（Settings） -> **通用**（General）-> **显示钢琴窗中的所有音符标签**（Enable note labels in Piano roll）来显示/隐藏所有音符标签。

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252F0SWP2NEfjCH6YVxmhI7B%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ddf752e06-0ddc-423c-a88c-2ecdda991c28&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=24123967&sv=2)

启用了标签显示的竖排钢琴键

您可以高亮单个音高、和弦和音阶。所有这些选项都可以由上下文菜单提供，方法是右键单击左侧竖排钢琴的某个键，然后在上下文菜单中选择 **您想要的高亮方式** 。

下面是您可以选择的高亮方式：

*   **标记音阶、和弦、各级的八度音或单个音。**
    
*   **取消标记各级的八度音、单个音或所有音高。**
    
*   **select all corresponding octave semitones.**
    

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FUPe7uJYTgi2ZVYzwXLY1%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D4d6da4e1-5b6e-4c85-aa33-be56b989ba05&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1a1b3980&sv=2)

标记了所有 C 和弦组成音的竖排钢琴键

若要标记/取消标记音阶/和弦，您首先要在钢琴窗的工具栏中选择一个音阶/和弦类型。

和弦与音阶分别从其根音和第一级音开始进行标记。

# 2.1 インストール | 日本語 | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/1.md)
.

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/1#id-1)

2.1.1 最小のシステム要件


---------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **OS:** Windows 7, MacOS X Lion, Linux
    
*   **CPU:** 1.5 GHz , 2コア の x86 , x86\_64 , ARM プロセッサ
    
*   **RAM:** 1 GB
    
*   **必要な空き容量:** 100 MB
    

これ以下の性能のパソコンでは動くかもしれませんが、動作の保証はできません

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/1#id-2)

2.1.2 推奨のシステム要件


---------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **OS:** Windows 10, MacOS X High Sierra, Linux
    
*   **CPU:** 2.0 GHz , 4コア の x86 , x86\_64 , ARM プロセッサ
    
*   **RAM:** 4 GB
    
*   **必要な空き容量:** 512 MB
    

これ以上の性能のパソコンではより快適にLMMSを動かすことができます

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/1#id-3)

2.1.3 LMMS の インストール


-------------------------------------------------------------------------

LMMSは [Windows](https://lmms.io/download/#windows)
, [macOS](https://lmms.io/download/#mac)
, [Linux](https://lmms.io/download/#linux)
 にインストール可能です

LMMSは多くのLinuxディストリビューションで動かせるように、 AppImage形式のパッケージが [lmms.io/download](https://lmms.io/download/)
 からダウンロードできます

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/1#id-6)

2.1.4 ソースコードからコンパイルしてインストールする


-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[LMMS development wiki on GitHub](https://github.com/LMMS/lmms/wiki/Compiling)
 に Linux 向けにLMMSをコンパイルしてインストールする方法が書かれています

[Previous1\. LMMSにようこそ](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp)
[Next2.2 セットアップ](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2)

Last updated 6 years ago

# BitInvader | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/bitinvader.md)
.

BitInvader 插件是一个定制化的波表合成器。

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FbO0aEuRHezmXgygmzLAf%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D3ad03738-3a20-46d2-9147-e771ca439249&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=dc9adcb4&sv=2)

BitInvader

*   **插值**（Interpolation）：对波形进行插值，或者说平滑。
    
*   **归一化**（Normalize）：归一化输出信号（移除 DC 偏移）。
    
*   **Length**（长度）旋钮用于调整波形采样的长度，值域为 8 到 128。
    
*   右下角标有“S”的按钮用于 **平滑**（Smooth）波形。
    
*   你可以在波形图象上 **自行绘制** 波形。
    
*   你可以在波形图象的右下角，“S”按钮旁边看到一排事先准备好的波形预置，点击想要的波形预置，波形图象将会自动应用你选择的预置：
    
    *   正弦（Sine）
        
    *   三角（triangle）
        
    *   锯齿（saw）
        
    *   方波（square）
        
    *   白噪（white-noise）
        
    *   自定义形状（user-defined shape）
        
    

[PreviousAudioFileProcessor](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/audiofileprocessor)
[NextFreeBoy](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/freeboy)

# Kicker | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/kicker.md)
.

Kicker 是一个鼓/低音合成器。

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FCuZsJ9PP52n30HUzpnNZ%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D5374e0e7-d0ca-470e-b0f8-2b953c41cca8&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=30d8a2df&sv=2)

Kicker

这个插件通过改变一个三角波的频率来工作。

*   **Start** 和 **End** 旋钮用于控制声音的起始频率和结束频率。留意这两个旋钮只有在下方对应的 LED 灯亮起时才管用。
    
*   **Start 是音符的 Start**：如果打开了 Start 的 LED 灯，那么你在钢琴窗编辑的所有音符，其起音都将是 Start 的频率。
    
*   **End 是音符的 End**：如果打开了 End 的 LED 灯，那么你在钢琴窗编辑的所有音符，其尾音都将是 End 的频率。
    
*   **Freq. slope：**调整频率包络的陡度，值越大频率滑得越久。
    
*   **Click**：负责增加鼓声音发出的振幅瞬态，以此增大冲击感（click/punch）。
    
*   **Start** 和 **End Distortion**：简单的失真包络。
    
*   **Gain：**对输出音频进行额外的增益处理（把声音放大）。
    
*   **Length：**音量包络的长度。
    
*   **Envelope Slope：**改变音量包络的陡度。
    
*   **Noise：**调整白噪声在信号中的占比。
    

Kicker 即鼓合成器，它最适合用在样式编辑器中。

如果你希望借助 Kicker 得到一个鼓，应该将 Start 旋钮设调到一个比较低的值，再将 End 旋钮调到比 Start 更低的值，并相应调低 Length 旋钮。

[PreviousGIG Player](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/gig-player)
[NextLB302](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/lb302)

# AudioFileProcessor | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/audiofileprocessor.md)
.

AudioFileProcessor（直译为”音频文件处理器“）插件用于 **播放采样** 。它支持以下音频格式：.wav、.ogg（Ogg Vorbis）、.ds（[DrumSynth](https://sourceforge.net/projects/drumsynth/)
 文件）、.flac、.spx（Speex）、.voc、.aif、.aiff、.au（Audacity BlockFile）、.raw。

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FlXFAtw3hd4jfSAbBrNfe%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3De2621fb1-9045-40ab-99d4-cce6e1412197&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=855b7afb&sv=2)

AudioFileProcessor

当弹奏不同琴键时，它会通过加快或减慢采样的速度来改变采样的频率，从而以不同的音高播放采样，当然，这也会改变采样的长度及其声音特征，使得声音在更高的音高更”快“（反之同理）。所以使用 AudioFileProcessor 来处理传统乐器的单音采样时，如果弹奏太高（或太低）的音符可能会得到不大理想的效果。 基音（basenote）定义了播放采样正常声音的琴键。默认情况下，这个琴键是 A4，也就是说弹奏 A4 键就是播放原始采样。你可以通过左右拖动（或直接点击移动）琴键上方的小白块来更改基音。

来看一下 AudioFileProcessor 的控件。

*   你可以使用 **插值模式** 下拉框来选择采样的插值方式，支持 不插值（None）、线性插值（Linear）和辛格插值（Sinc）。
    

如果设置插值模式为不插值（None），可能导致采样率低的采样发出奇怪的声音（尤其是以较低的音高弹奏时），所以我们不推荐你选这个不插值。如果你使用非常高的基音，选择插值可能更好。而不插值反而更适用于噪声打击乐器上，因为它能产生更尖锐的噪声。

*   文件夹图标（📂）用于打开要载入的采样文件。你也可以通过把 LMMS 侧边栏的采样文件直接拖到乐器窗口（须保持在插件〔PLUGIN〕选项卡中）来载入采样。
    
*   响度（Amplify）旋钮（图标是一个宽直角三角形）用于更改采样音频的播放音量，以原有音量的百分比表示。100% 为原有音量，0% 表示静音，200% 表示原有音量的两倍。拉到 100% 以下会降低音量，但这不会修复采样中的削波。
    
*   **起始点**（Startpoint）旋钮（“|←”，图标是一个到顶的左箭头）和 **终止点**（Endpoint）旋钮（“→|”，图标是一个到顶的右箭头）用于限定采样只播放哪个区间，采样预览中有两个突出的竖线，以此方便你直观地编辑采样区间。这两条竖线默认放在采样的最两端。
    
*   **循环点**（Loopback point）旋钮（►|◄，图标是两个指向中心竖线的小三角）用于设置循环区间。在采样预览中表现为一根更淡的竖线，它只能介于两条突出线之间。在循环播放时，采样将只在循环点到终止点之间循环。循环点默认与起始点重合。
    
*   **倒放**（Reverse sample）按钮（图标是一个反 R）决定了采样播放的**方向**（即是否倒放）。当此按钮亮起时，采样将反着播放。
    
*   **跨音符播放**（Continue sample playback across notes）按钮（在倒放音频按钮下面，图标是两个水平矩形，上面画了个小三角指针），点亮后，采样可以以一种“卡顿推进”的感觉进行播放。当你启用这个按钮，每次弹奏，采样总会从它上一次结束的地方继续播放。这个按钮默认是关闭的，也就是说每次弹奏新的音符，采样都是从起始点开始播放。在启用本按钮的情况下，你可以通过在钢琴窗里插一个非常低的音符（比如 D0 及以下），如果你中途没有改变基音，那么播放位置将回到起始点。当然了，如果你不想这样，也可以使用自动化来控制这个按钮关闭再打开，以此让播放位置回到采样的起始点。
    

如果你弹奏了一个比基音低超过 53 个半音（加 51 音分）的音符，会触发采样的重新开始播放。举个例子，如果你使用 AudioFileProcessor 且保持基音默认为 A4，那么任何低于 E0（不包括 E0）的音符就不是让采样从上次断掉的地方继续播放了，而是会在该音符之后回到起始点。而这个很低的音符不会发出任何声音。

如果你的乐器本身 detune 被降低了 52 音分或更多，那么 E0 本身也就是比基音低超过 53 个半音再低 51 音分的音符了，所以也不会发出任何声音，而会像上述一样触发这种“重置播放位置”的功能。

题外话，这些音符行为只会在这种”重置播放位置“的模式下出现，所以如果你发现 AudioFileProcessor 中的低音符**没有发出**预期的**声音**时，请检查是不是误开了跨音符播放模式。

出现这种情况的原因是：在假设基准音为标准的 A4（440 Hz）的前提下，低于 E0 再减去 51 音分的频率会低于 20 Hz，从而超出人类听觉的理论范围。当然，这只是一个假设；如果用户调整了基准音（无论升高还是降低），很可能是因为采样本身的音高并不正好是 A4。在这些情况下，当基准音较高时，一些本来合理但相对较低的音符可能会变得无法播放；而当基准音低于预期时，也可能更容易触发这种重置卡顿模式。

接下来看看右边那组按钮。这组按钮决定了采样的 **循环控制** 。你在同一时间只能点亮这三个按钮中的一个。

*   **禁用循环（Disable loop）** 即上部分图标为电源开关符号的按钮，它表示禁用循环。
    
*   **启用循环（Enable loop）** 即左下部分图标为闭合循环曲线箭头的按钮，它表示当音符持续按下时让采样以循环点为起始，到终止点之间重复播放。这么说，如果循环点和起始点重合，那就相当于采样整体不断循环，直到音符松开。
    
*   **启用乒乓循环（Enable ping-pong loop）** 即右下部分图标为垂直分成两半的对称三角形（◿◺）的按钮，它表示在循环点和终止点之间来回播放。 也就是说，如果一直不松开音符，那么采样会首先正常播放，到达终止点后，在那里它会向后倒放，直到回到循环点——如此往复。

# GIG Player | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/gig-player.md)
.

GIG Player，或者说 GigaSampler 播放器，这个插件用于在 LMMS 载入 GigaSampler 文件（.gig）。

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252F2E5ecpjPafN0yBAb4qZn%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc46f49af-b3f8-4f69-8840-dccb4053f22a&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9f2bc368&sv=2)

GIG Player

GIG 和声音字体（soundfont）有些相似，只不过它是在 GigaSampler 和 GigaStudio 中使用的。.gig 文件是最基本的乐器单元，可以作为一个乐器载入。这些乐器文件实际上是一个采样集（包括用于支持播放的数据），乐器就是依赖这些数据运作起来的。一个 GIG 文件支持最多 128 个乐器。

顺带一提，借助软件和扩展，一些 GIG 插件可以支持键位切换技法（keyswitch）。不幸的是，目前 LMMS 的 GIG Player 还未支持键位切换。GIG 文件会像对待各个 MIDI 库一样对待自己的乐器，LMMS 则会载入 GIG 文件中的乐器并以音色库（patches）的形式展示给用户。

GIG 文件格式现今已经过时，相关的乐器文件也不再被广泛使用。

.sfz 文件支持 GIG 乐器的大多数功能，所以我们更建议使用声音字体（SoundFont）文件和 SFZ 文件。

LMMS 通过 LinuxSampler 的 libgig 来支持 GIG 文件。

[PreviousFreeBoy](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/freeboy)
[NextKicker](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/kicker)

# 导出你的歌曲 | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/bian-qu/dao-chu-ni-de-ge-qu.md)
.

如果要成功导出一首曲子，你需要考虑很多因素。比如计算机性能、存储空间、还有你需要的歌曲质量等。 你会在导出窗口进行一些设置。如果你的计算机有更多的性能和存储，也就可以选择导出更优质的歌曲。当然了，它也会消耗更多的导出时间。

并没有什么快捷简单的经验能让你一直遵循，我们都是靠自己最合理的猜测来配置选项的。设想你导好了一首曲子，然后试听。如果它不好听怎么办？答案是：删掉它，下一次导出变一下导出配置再导出一次。 导出歌曲不会更改也不会破坏你的 .mmpz 工程文件，导出只会读你的工程文件，就好像把CD读进计算机里一样。所以放心去改吧，直到它符合你的预期！

如果不认真对待导出设置，那就很容易感觉曲子一些地方不对劲，就好像弹错什么东西一样。幸运的是，我们一般只需要几次试错就可以摸清曲子适合什么导出配置。如果认真对待导出，那么你的作品就不会出什么问题，甚至可能会更好。;)

我们建议您更新 LMMS 到最新版本。

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/bian-qu/dao-chu-ni-de-ge-qu#ru-he-dao-chu-ge-qu)

如何导出歌曲


------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

有多种方法导出你的歌曲： 点击工具栏中的导出按钮（ ![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252Fsydkdk0X8OlPHudIru9O%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D28a3c8f3-81e6-47d0-9eef-8bdf5db59e90&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ae735fd&sv=2) ），或者按 Ctrl+E ，抑或者点击主菜单栏的 文件 → 导出。执行操作后，你会唤出一个文件选择窗口，在这里可以命名导出的歌曲，并选择一个导出目录。

以上都完成后，项目导出窗口就出现了。这个窗口会列出所有可供调整的导出配置选项。一切妥当后，点击导出就可以了。

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/bian-qu/dao-chu-ni-de-ge-qu#wen-jian-ge-shi-file-format)

文件格式（File Format）


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

有两个格式可供选择：

*   **WAV**，或者说波形音频格式，往往是新用户导出歌曲的最佳选择。因为它兼容大多数计算机和多媒体设备，而无需进行格式转换。
    
*   **OGG** Vorbis 格式是一种开源的有损压缩音频格式，它更适用于对质量有特殊要求（比如进行简单分享）的用户，OGG 对设备的兼容性比 WAV 差。
    

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/bian-qu/dao-chu-ni-de-ge-qu#cai-yanglsample-rate)

采样率（Sample Rate）


-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

这个选项表示歌曲每秒被采样的次数。 数值越大，歌曲中越多的变化就会被记录到导出音频中。换句话说，如果你的歌曲有很多细微的动态，那么你应该把这个数值调大些。 在大部分情况下，保持该选项默认即可。 44.1 kHz（千赫兹）便是通常 CD 使用的采样率。如果采样率过低，可能会导致歌曲失真、杂音；如果采样率过高，则会导致你的音频文件过大，导出过程也会变得很长。

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/bian-qu/dao-chu-ni-de-ge-qu#bi-telbit-rate)

比特率（Bit Rate）


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

比特率（或称位速率）即每秒处理的二进制位数，是指设备读取歌曲时处理歌曲的数据量（或处理歌曲的速度）。这是一个很重要的选项，因为它将同时决定你歌曲的质量和大小。调越高，歌曲的质量越好，但也会更大。下表列出了一些常见的比特率数值（kbps：千比特每秒）：

比特率

应用实例

64 kbps

调幅（AM）收音机

128 kbps

调频（FM）收音机

160 kbps

经过压缩的 MP3

192 kbps

数字广播

256 kbps

高质量 MP3

320 kbps

CD 音质

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/bian-qu/dao-chu-ni-de-ge-qu#wei-shen-bit-depth)

位深（Bit Depth）


------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FIoEmTJv40tA8Pc9FGaBq%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0a045aad-6b0c-4a2a-be9e-3fc8f854d697&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=11bd37dd&sv=2)

位深度为 3 时的图象示例

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/bian-qu/dao-chu-ni-de-ge-qu#yin-yue-shang-kan)

音乐上看

位深是歌曲声音中不同音量的数量。如果一首歌是 3 比特的，那么它就支持 23 = 8 个音量级别，也就是有 8 个分辨率的位深。 众所周知，声音波形是必须有上下起伏，即既有波峰又有波谷的——并且是对称展开在 X 轴周围的，也就是说必须把 8 除以 2 才能得到你听到的声音的音量级别。正如上图所示，X 轴上/下其实只有 4 个音量级别。因此，当位深为 3 时，最弱的声音则表示为振幅为 1 的波形。图中所表示的最大音量对应的振幅为 4。 动态范围与位深相关。

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/bian-qu/dao-chu-ni-de-ge-qu#ji-suan-ji-ke-xue-shang-kan)

计算机科学上看

这是计算机处理器在歌曲编码的过程中，以科学计数法或整数格式与程序进行交互的一种方式。选择 32 位浮点数可以让计算机更精确地处理（编译）歌曲，但也会消耗更多的性能。使用 16 位整数则可以实现更快的处理速度，尤其是在较旧或性能较弱的计算机上。一般来说，为了获得更好的音质，这个设置应当设为 32 位浮点数。

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/bian-qu/dao-chu-ni-de-ge-qu#cha-zhi-interpolation)

插值（Interpolation）


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

插值和回归（regression）的含义相同，即平滑绘制声波的程度。当 LMMS 获取到歌曲中的两个采样点，它可以选择在这两个点之间绘制一条直线，也可以选择使用一些算法来智能绘制平滑的曲线。这样可以模拟声音本身就具有的自然曲线，逻辑上来说这应该会得到更棒的声音。 如果你想这样插值，请始终选择“Sinc best” 选项。还有，这个选项不影响最终导出歌曲的大小。

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/bian-qu/dao-chu-ni-de-ge-qu#yi-xie-you-yong-de-zi-yuan)

一些有用的资源


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   [了解采样率、位深和插值的概念](http://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W2-FP7twy8s%7C)
    
*   [了解比特率等更多概念](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HqHIOA-Fcuw%7C)

Patman is a GUS-patch (.PAT) player.

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252F4qkmrQW2MprdzTNpyvQ4%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D13647c66-808a-4129-b46d-615f8c629ef3&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=455403d&sv=2)

PatMan

GUS patches are patches associated with Gravis Ultrasound soundcards. Their concept is very similar to SoundFont and they can be considered SoundFont predecessors.

Patman is a simple patch loader. It also allows looping of the patch. There aren't many patches online, but you can find a pretty good collection [here](http://freepats.zenvoid.org/)
.

# SF2 Player | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/sf2-player.md)
.

Sf2 Player, or rather the SoundFont Player, uses SoundFont files (.sf2).

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252Fg6PRWPBRtPEHFaKmzzwg%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dd5af0afa-98c4-4bff-b246-d95ee0834863&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f28c3e16&sv=2)

SF2 Player

There are no SoundFont files bundled with LMMS, but you can find a lot of SoundFont files on the internet (see ). A SoundFont bank contains base samples in PCM format (as used in WAV files) that are mapped to sections on a MIDI keyboard. This is especially useful for people working with MIDI files, since many SoundFont files contain samples of real instruments. You can select the default SoundFont file that will be used when loading MIDI files in the Settings. It is recommended to use a SoundFont file that follows the General MIDI (GM) standard as the default soundfont.

Many SoundFont files have multiple patches (multiple types of sounds), often set up as different instruments or different styles of instruments. To select a patch you can use the patch and bank spinners, or click on the wrench icon to open a window where you can see all banks and patches.

Because the Sf2 Player is a MIDI-based instrument, you cannot use ENV/LFO directly in the instrument interface. This tab is intentionally left empty.

Some SoundFont files support reverb and chorus. Reverb and Chorus can be applied directly in the plugin. SF2 player has four knobs for controlling each effect:

*   Reverb:
    
    *   The **SIZE** knob lets you select the room size for the reverb.
        
    *   The **DAMP** knob controls the damping. Damping set to 1 will cut the reverb when the note is released.
        
    *   The **WIDTH** knob controls the stereo width.
        
    *   The **LEVEL** knob controls the wet level.
        
    
*   Chorus:
    
    *   The **NUM** knob controls the number of voices in the chorus. This represents how many individual signals are being processed.
        
    *   The **LEVEL** knob controls the wet level of the chorus effect. If it is set to 0 you only hear the original sound.
        
    *   The **SPD** knob controls the speed (rate) of the LFO.
        
    *   The **DEPTH** knob controls the depth of the modulation. Higher values increase detuning.
        
    

[PreviousPatMan](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/patman)
[Nextsfxr](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/sfxr)

# LB302 | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/lb302.md)
.

LB302 是对 TB-303 不完整的单音模拟。

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252F2XN8PBFAfuHzGlsnxzed%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D2952ab06-2bc4-4770-a483-0058bf0412ce&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=bd23418f&sv=2)

LB302

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/lb302#vco-ya-kong-zhen-dang-qi-voltagecontrolled-oscillator)

VCO（压控振荡器，Voltage-Controlled Oscillator）


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

你可以为这个振荡器选择以下不同的波形：锯齿波、三角波、方波、圆角方波、“Moog式”波形、正弦波、指数波形和白噪声。

SLIDE 用于调整滑音衰减。

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/lb302#vcf-ya-konglbo-qi-voltagecontrolled-filter)

VCF（压控滤波器，Voltage-Controlled Filter）


-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **24dB**（每倍频程衰减24分贝）
    
*   **CUT**（滤波，Cutoff）
    
*   **RES**（共鸣，Resonance）
    
*   **ENV MOD**（包络调制量）
    
*   **DEC**（衰减，Decay）
    

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/lb302#dist-shi-zhen-distortion)

DIST（失真，Distortion）


------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   Slide
    
*   Dead
    
*   DIST

# OpulenZ | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/opulenz.md)
.

OpulenZ is an emulation of the Yamaha YM3812, a.k.a. OPL2, FM synth chip which was used i sound cards around 1990 - the Adlib and Sound Blasters. It generates sound through frequency modulation or adding 2 oscillators.

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FuL0R2kKoyOr1QNePDbHS%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D3a747e87-ecc1-4ef1-b2a3-c77eb5bf3d31&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2134540c&sv=2)

OpulenZ

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/opulenz#operators)

Operators


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The two oscillators are called operators. In FM mode (bottom left), operator 1 modulates operator 2. Otherwise both operators are routed to the output.

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/opulenz#controls)

Controls


-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

For each operator, the controls are:

*   **ATT, DEC, SUS, REL:** an ADSR envelope
    
*   **KSR:** keyboard scaling rate. When KSR is on, envelopes get faster when the pitch rises.
    
*   **PERC:** percussive envelope. Makes the envelope go straight to release ignoring held keys.
    
*   **TREM:** applies tremolo (slight amplitude modulation) to the operator
    
*   **VIB:** applies vibrato (slight pitch modulation) to the operator
    
*   **FEEDBACK:** only for operator 1 - feeds a portion of the operator's output back into ir
    
*   **LVL:** the output level of the operator
    
*   **SCALE:** determines the scaling applied to the output level when the pitch rises
    
*   **MUL:** the frequency multiplier for the operator. 1 stands for the standard pitch played on the keyboard, 0 is one octave below.
    
*   **WAVEFORM:** selection from 4 waveforms: sine, half sine, absolute sine and quarter sine. NB: a DC removal plugin might be a good idea when routing waveforms other than sine to the output.
    

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/opulenz#global-controls)

Global Controls

*   **FM:** when this is on, frequency modulation is used. Otherwise the operators are mixed together.
    
*   **DEPTH, TREM and VIBR:** these select deeper tremolo and vibrato. Applies to both operators.
    

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/opulenz#useful-resources)

Useful Resources


---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Getting started with FM: [http://www.gorenfeld.net/lou/fmguide.html](http://www.gorenfeld.net/lou/fmguide.html)

Programming the AdLib/Sound Blaster FM Music Chips: [http://www.shipbrook.net/jeff/sb.html](http://www.shipbrook.net/jeff/sb.html)
 (some of the controls are presented inverted in LMMS, to fit better with other symbols)

[PreviousNEScaline](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/nescaline)
[NextOrganic](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/organic)

Last updated 6 months ago

# NEScaline | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/nescaline.md)
.

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FcIBgLL2KdihsagEvhmCL%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3De52262c0-5128-4371-adc2-9c6a466de17b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1ae681e3&sv=2)

NEScaline

NEScaline 是一个红白机（NES）音效合成器。它提供了四个音频通道（channels），每个通道附带了不同的噪声和设置。

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/nescaline#yong-fa)

用法


------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   MASTER 下的 VOL 控件控制乐器的主音量，值取自 0 ~ 2。默认为 1，即标准音量。
    
*   MASTER 下的 VIBR 控件控制乐器的主颤音效果，值取自 0 ~ 15。
    
*   VOL（音量）取自 0~15。
    
*   CRS（音高粗调）允许你在 -24 ～ 24 的范围间粗略地改变声音的的音高。
    
*   ENV（包络）提供了一个包络来让声音变得更短。
    
    *   无限（infinity，∞）按钮。启用后，包络会被无限循环，在音符输入时提供反复的包络输出。
        
    *   LEN（包络线长度，envelope length）决定包络持续的时间长度，在声音切断前确定其长度。值取自 0~15。
        
    *   WIDTH（执行周期，duty cycle）
        
    
*   SWEEP 提供了扫频效果。具体是基于你给的扫频范围来向前/向后改变音高。
    
    *   AMT（扫频范围）决定最终的音高，值取自 -7~7。
        
    *   RATE（扫频速率）决定到达最终音高的时间。值越大时间越久。
        
    

部分通道有一些相同的旋钮和参数。

每个通道输出一组声音波形，并可由上述设置项进行控制。

*   CH1（通道一）和 CH2（通道二）提供半方波，你可以利用这两个通道轻松地混出有特色的声音。
    
*   CH3（通道三）提供了一个三角波。
    
*   CH4（通道四）专门提供方波形噪声。这个通道有专用的控件，来调制和使之跟随输入音符作为噪声的基频。
    

[PreviousMonstro](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/monstro)
[NextOpulenZ](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/opulenz)

# 2.2 セットアップ | 日本語 | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2.md)
.

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-1)

2.2.1 一般設定


----------------------------------------------------------------

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-1-1)

2.2.1.1 バッファサイズ

この設定はとても大事です。バッファサイズはLMMSから出力される音の遅延を調整します(バッファサイズが低ければ低いほど低遅延です)。しかし、小さすぎる値を設定すると、使用しているコンピュータによっては、音がぷつぷつとなったり、途切れたり、鳴らなかったりして、LMMSが使えなくなる可能性があります。

性能の高いコンピュータではバッファサイズを下げても大丈夫でしょう。ですが、ポップ音が鳴ったり、音がぷつぷつとなったり、途切れたり、鳴らなかったりする場合には、バッファサイズが低い可能性が高いのでバッファサイズを上げましょう。 詳しくは、([2.3.1.1](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/3#1-1)
)を見てください。

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-1-2)

2.2.1.2 Misc

*   **ツールチップを有効にする**
    
*   **設定を変更した後に再起動のメッセージを表示する**
    
*   **dB単位でボリュームを表示する**
    
*   **デフォルトでプロジェクトファイルの圧縮を有効にする(.mmpz形式)**
    
*   **One instrument track window mode:**
    
*   **高音質(HQ-mode)で音を出力する**
    
*   **コンパクトなボタンを使う**
    
*   **VSTプラグインをホストのプレイバックと同期させる**
    
*   **ピアノロール内のノートのラベルを有効にする**
    
*   **波形を表示するディスプレイを有効にする**
    
*   **音が鳴っていない状態でもエフェクトを有効にした状態のままにする**
    
*   **プロジェクトファイルをセーブしたときにバックアップを作成する**
    
*   **LMMSの起動時に、前回最後に開いていたプロジェクトファイルを開く**
    

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-1-3)

2.2.1.3 プラグインの埋め込み

*   **埋め込まない**
    
*   **QT Apiを使用して埋め込む**
    
*   **Win32 Apiを利用して埋め込む**
    

Win32 Apiを利用するオプションはWindows版のみ選択できます

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-1-4)

2.2.1.4 言語

LMMSに表示される言語を選択します。

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-2)

2.2.2 ディレクトリ


------------------------------------------------------------------

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-2-1)

2.2.2.1 作業ディレクトリ

ユーザーが使うサンプル、プロジェクト、プリセットなどが保存されるディレクトリです

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-2-2)

2.2.2.2 GIG ディレクトリ

GIG ファイルが置いてあるディレクトリを指定します

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-2-3)

2.2.2.3 SF2 ディレクトリ

`.sf2` ファイルが置いてあるディレクトリを指定します。 基本的には、作業ディレクトリの中を指定します。

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-2-4)

2.2.2.4 VST プラグイン ディレクトリ

VST(2.4)プラグインが置いてあるフォルダを指定します。32bit,64bitのプラグインが対象です。

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-2-5)

2.2.2.5 LADSPA プラグイン ディレクトリ

LADSPAプラグインが置いてあるフォルダを指定します。

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-2-6)

2.2.2.6 STK RawWave ディレクトリ

STK RawWave ファイルが置いてあるフォルダを指定します。

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-2-7)

2.2.2.7 デフォルト サウンドフォント ファイル

SF2プレイヤー[(see 5.13)](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/5/13)
をロードした際に初めに読み込む `.sf2` ファイルを指定します。(任意)

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-2-8)

2.2.2.8 テーマ ディレクトリ

Despite the name, this is actually where the _current_ theme is stored. To change themes, set this path to the location of the desired theme itself.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-2-9)

2.2.2.9 背景画像

This is an image used for the background of the main LMMS window. Leave it blank to use the image provided by the current theme.

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-3)

2.2.3 パフォーマンス設定「


----------------------------------------------------------------------

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-3-1)

2.2.3.1 自動保存

*   **Auto-save interval:** 自動保存の間隔をスライドして設定します
    
*   **自動保存を有効にする:** This setting allows LMMS to periodically save a backup of your currently open project to help protect against data loss in the event of power outage, user error, etc.
    
*   **再生中に自動保存を有効にする:** この設定が有効の時、再生中でも自動保存を行います。
    

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-3-2)

2.2.3.2 UI エフェクト

*   **ソングエディタをスムーズにスクロールできるようにする:**
    
*   **AudioFileProcessorでプレイバックカーソルを表示する:**
    

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-4)

2.2.4 Audio 設定


--------------------------------------------------------------------

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-4-1)

2.2.4.1 Audio Interface

この項目はLMMSがどの方法で音を出すのか設定します

mac OS と Windowsでは, **SDL** が推奨されています.

*   **Dummy (no sound output):**
    
*   **SDL (Simple DirectMedia Layer):** SDL only has one option, a text box where you can specify the device. If you leave this blank, a device will be selected automatically.
    
*   **PortAudio**†**:**
    

†この設定はWindows版のLMMSのみ変更可能です

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-5)

2.2.5 MIDI設定


------------------------------------------------------------------

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jp/2/2#id-5-1)

2.2.5.1 MIDI インターフェース

*   **Dummy (no MIDI support):**
    
*   **WinMM MIDI**†:
    

† この設定はWindows版のLMMSのみ変更可能です

# Mallets | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/mallets.md)
.

Mallets 是一个基于采样的合成器，你可以用它来模拟一些悦耳的敲击声 (#w#)ii

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FMIS6JaGZNR1NcjhIEhAy%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D26519072-7f8f-4ace-adaa-0e21a9cdcd49&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b63142b3&sv=2)

Mallets

有三个不同的门类供你选择，针对你选择的声音，可以调整不同的设置项。

1.  Percussion instruments: Marimba, Vibraphone, Agogo, Wood1, Reso, Wood2, Beats, Two Fixed and Clumps
    
2.  Tubular bells
    
3.  Bars: Uniform Bar, Tuned Bar, Glass and Tibetan Bowl
    

你可以通过点击棒槌图片旁边的下拉对话框来改变这些门类，左键单击是下一个，右键单击是上一个。你也可以通过直接点击右边的箭头按钮来直接浏览所有可选的声音。

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/mallets#percussion-instrument-settings)

Percussion Instrument Settings


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   Hardness
    
*   Position
    
*   Vib Gain
    
*   Vib Freq
    
*   Stick Mix
    
*   Spread
    

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/mallets#tubular-bells-settings)

Tubular Bells Settings


---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   Modulator
    
*   Crossfade
    
*   LFO Speed
    
*   LFO Depth
    
*   ADSR
    
*   Spread
    

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/mallets#bar-settings)

Bar Settings


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   Bowed (on/off)
    
*   Pressure
    
*   Motion
    
*   Speed
    
*   Vibrato
    
*   Spread

# Monstro | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/monstro.md)
.

Monstro 是一个原生的 LMMS 合成器乐器插件。

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FEZ1gCOwnHSyVijlTxdZy%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ddf70f224-2e31-452c-ba9f-61b7ecf14bec&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a8c2d661&sv=2)

Monstro

顺带一提，在这个乐器的右下角有一排调制模式开关，它用于控制 OSC2 和 OSC3 的关系。其中包括以下几个可选项：

*   MIX：OSC2 和 OSC3 单纯混合在一起。
    
*   AM：使用 OSC2 调制 OSC3 的振幅（音量）。
    
*   FM：使用 OSC2 调制 OSC3 的频率（音高）。（采用相位来调制，以防止音高偏移。）
    
*   PM：通过 OSC2 的相位调制 OSC3。
    

如果没有处在 MIX 模式，那么你将不再能听到 OSC2，因为 OSC2 去调制 OSC3 的声音了。

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/monstro#operators-mo-kuai-shi-tu)

OPERATORS（模块视图）


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

LFO 是 Low Frequency Oscillator（低频振荡器）的缩写。乐器中 LFO1 和 LFO2 是完全相同的两个模块，它们无法发出声音，只会影响声音的生成。每个 LFO 有三个旋钮：

*   **Rate** 旋钮控制 LFO 的速度（频率），单位为毫秒。这里的速度说的是单个周期的长度，你可以通过这个公式来换算为赫兹（Hz）：Hz = 1 / ( 旋钮的值 / 1000 )。
    
*   **ATT** 控制 LFO 的音头（attack）的长度，通常来说，你可以通过这个旋钮来增加更多的 attack，以此让 LFO 不是立即开始工作，而是慢慢开始。
    
*   **PHS** 控制 LFO 的相位。这个旋钮不会影响 **随机**（Random）或 **随机平滑**（Random Smooth）模式。
    

你可以为每个 LFO 选一个波形。请注意，这些波形都不是带限的（band-limited）。它们和一般的振荡器的非带限波形几乎一样，只有两点不同：随机（Random）和随机平滑（Random Smooth）。

随机（Random）使用一种带形状的噪声来实现：LFO 会生成一个随机值来按周期改变波形（周期大小由 RATE 旋钮决定）。

随机平滑（Random Smooth）也是一样的，只是 LFO 的值会在随机值之间**平滑**变化（使用余弦插值），这意味着值之间的每个变化都呈现出半个正弦波的形状。

包络（ENVs）有七个旋钮：

*   **PRE：**预延迟（pre-delay）——包络起效前为 0（静音）的时间长度。
    
*   **ATT：**起音（attack）——包络从 0 到 1 的时间长度。
    
*   **HOLD：**保持——包络保持在 1 的时间长度。
    
*   **DEC：**衰减（decay）——包络从 1 降至 0 的时间长度。不过如果持续（sustain）大于 0，那么衰减过程会提前终止，并停留在持续电平。
    
*   **SUS：**持续（sustain）——包络持续阶段的电平。也就是说，衰减阶段结束后，包络会停留在该电平，直到松开音符。
    
*   **REL：**释放（release）——同样是以从 1 滑至 0 的时长来表示。不过，如果持续（sustain）低于 1，实际的释放时间会更短。也就是说，释放阶段表示的是在松开音符之后包络从 持续（sustain）滑至 0 的时长。
    
*   **SLOPE：**包络（起音、衰减和释放阶段）曲线的形状。如果值是负的，那么包络曲线往下弯，反之往上弯。如果为 0 就是斜直线。
    

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/monstro#matrix-ju-zhen-shi-tu)

MATRIX（矩阵视图）


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

矩阵视图包含了 11 个调制对象，每个都可以借由 4 个调制器任意调制。

所有 3 个振荡器有下面三个调制对象：

*   **音量**（Volume）：保留一个特殊的包络来专门控制音量。在这个音量包络中，调制总是以乘法方式进行，即包络永远不会让音量超出它的原始值。音量包络经过归一化处理，并对峰值部分进行了反向映射，因此 Attack 阶段会从原始音量开始，并逐渐向下变化；当包络值为 0 时，原始音量就会被完全保留（不被削减）。LFO 对音量的作用是相对的——一个调制值为 0.5 的 LFO 会在原始音量的 0.5 到 1.5（1 ± 0.5）倍之间变化。负值会反转 LFO 的波形方向。
    
*   **相位**（Phase）：无论包络还是 LFO，其最大调制范围均为 360°。包络相位总是从 +0° 的相位偏移出发，然后向上或向下运动（这取决于调制值）。LFO 会在正负之间的相位偏移上振荡，但是区间相同——在调制值拉最大的时候，他们会以 -180° 到 +180° 的范围振荡。
    
*   **音高**（Pitch）：包络和 LFO 对音高的最大区间是 2 个八度。刻度采用对数形式，所以 0.5 对应一个八度， 0.25 对应 6 个半音（半个八度），以此类推。音高被限制于最小值（18 Hz）和最大值（48 kHz）之间，如果包络或 LFO 让音高超出这个区间，超出的频率会被削掉。
    

此外，振荡器 1 和 3 有额外的调制对象：

*   **OSC1 的 PW**：PW 指脉冲宽度（占空比）。最大调制范围都是 100 个百分点（percentage points，pp），所以包络可以从 0pp 到 +100pp（或者如果调制值为负，那么是 0pp到 -100pp）。LFO 可以在 -50pp 到 +50pp 之间振荡。当然，最终调制结果会被限制在 PW 旋钮的最小值和最大值之间（0%～100%），不会超出这个范围。
    
*   **OSC3 SUB** 控制了两个波形的混合。数值和上述规律相同。
    

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/monstro#xiao-ti-shi)

小提示


---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Monstro 是一个复杂的合成器，它的某些设置会消耗更多的 CPU。越多调制器被使用，对CPU的占用率就越高。如果你将矩阵视图中的调制器设置为非零值，也会消耗更多的 CPU。

类似的，FM、AM 和 PM 模式也会比一般的 MIX 模式消耗略多的 CPU，因为前者需要更多的计算。

[PreviousMallets](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/mallets)
[NextNEScaline](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/nescaline)

Last updated 1 month ago

# Organic | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/organic.md)
.

Organic is an additive synthesizer which combins the output of up to 8 oscillators.

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FEzdoiFO47Omx3v45K2an%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D848d83f6-aa5b-4ce3-9d5a-eadbfa72cd18&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c6599cc1&sv=2)

Organic

Although it has more oscillators than TripleOscillator, it is less flexible because it lacks a lot of its abilities, among them the option to modulate the oscillators with each other. The phase offsets of each oscillator are randomized whenever a note is played (for both stereo channels separately). For that reason this instrument sounds slightly different everytime you press a key even when you don't change any settings.Organic is most useful for creating organ instruments (that's where its name comes from) and pads.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/organic#individual-oscillator-controls)

Individual Oscillator Controls

*   **Wave:** The waveform generated by the oscillator
    
    *   0%: Sine
        
    *   1%: Sawtooth
        
    *   2%: Pulse
        
    *   3%: Triangle
        
    *   4%: Moog Triangle
        
    *   5%: Exponential
        
    
*   **Vol:** Volume of the oscillator
    
*   **Pan:** Stereo panning of the oscillator
    
*   **Wide:** Coarse of the oscillator. Has a range of one octave up and down.
    

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/organic#general-controls)

General Controls

*   **Dist:** Adds a clipper effect (distortion)
    
*   **Vol:** General volume
    
*   **Randomize:** Randomizes all oscillator settings
    

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/organic#tuning-of-the-oscillators)

Tuning of the Oscillators

This is the default coarse of the oscillators with "Wide" on 0

# Triple Oscillator | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/triple-oscillator.md)
.

The TripleOscillator plugin is a synthesizer which generates sounds by combining the waveform of up to three function generators.

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252Fk0b5uz80sql9UDbdgipr%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0150974f-87c2-42a0-81c1-546e6e98573f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d4912e90&sv=2)

TripleOscillator

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/triple-oscillator#user-defined)

User-defined

Double-click to use any sound file as a waveform

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/triple-oscillator#tips)

Tips


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   The best results are reached by combining different waveforms with different chorus settings.
    
*   Most sounds created by a TripleOscillator sound boring and dull without an Envelope.
    
*   If you want to know more about basics of sound synthesis you may read this awesome article by Beau Sievers - [http://beausievers.com/synth/synthbasics/](http://beausievers.com/synth/synthbasics/)
    

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/triple-oscillator#making-patches)

Making Patches


---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

If you find all your instruments sound like an Atari 2600 (unless that's what you were going for), these guidelines for creating patches that sound like familiar musical instruments or sounds will help you. These instructions are intentionally a bit vague, so you can easily adapt them to the type of music you want to make. When making patches, it often helps to have a BB track playing the sound over and over as you edit.

When creating instruments with different generators, not just TripleOscillator patches, there are a few unwanted events that may occur. The most frequent problem is "clicks". The instrument sounds pretty much ok, but there's a distinct "click" artifact every time the note plays. Clicks are annoying, but most can be easily removed. The start-event clicks are the easiest.

1.  Open the ENV for VOLUME
    
2.  Set AMT to maximum
    
3.  Set ATTACK to just a little over 0 (try 0.04)
    

Listen to the sound. If your click artifact didn't change a little, then the click is from the "termination" of the sound. Here the situation is more difficult. You may be able to remove the click by giving REL <= 0.05.

Annother annoying problem is "noisy crater" randomly occurring in longer notes. This is often caused by too high ! in respect of either the cutoff Frq or the actual chosen filter. These type of artifacts can be seriously difficult to remove without changing the quality of the sound. Often success versus fiasco depends on minute alterations in Q.

If you are not familiar with terms like LFO, filters and resonance-Q amount, you should now go to the section Working with Instruments and read the paragraph The Env/LFO/Filter tab. All the basics (and more) are explained there.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/triple-oscillator#synth-bass)

Synth Bass

We'll start with your basic synthesized techno bass sound, because this is a relatively simple patch to start learning your way around the TripleOscillator. Start with a fresh TripleOscillator with the default sine wave patch.

1.  Start on the "PLUGIN" page and change the waveforms. Try some different combinations: a square, a triangle and a sine wave are a good combo, or set them all to saw or moog-saw.
    
2.  Select the "ENV/LFO" page. Turn the filter on to "LowPass", "2x LowPass" or "Moog" and turn the cutoff all the way down. Your sound becomes almost inaudible.
    
3.  Go to the "CUTOFF" sub-page and turn the envelope on. Turn "Hold", "Sustain" and "Release" to zero. Hear the filter now?
    
4.  Now start playing with the resonance knob in the "FILTER" section. If you're using the Moog filter, don't turn the resonance past about 0.85 - you've been warned!
    
5.  Now play some really low notes.
    

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/triple-oscillator#organ)

Organ

Real organs are basically additive synthesizers: they add together a few dozen simple sine waves to form a complex tone. This is pretty easy to mimic with the TripleOscillator and LMMS' chords.

1.  Start with a fresh sine patch, and using each oscillator's "Crs" (coarse) knobs, place them in 3 different octaves.
    
2.  Go to the "FUNC" page and turn on chords. Set the chord to "Octave" and set "RANGE" to 3 or 4. Neat, eh?
    
3.  Play different C's. They sound similar, don't they? That's because most of the harmonics are the same.
    
4.  For some fun, change on of the oscillators to a triangle wave.
    
5.  You can refine the sound by changing which octaves each oscillator plays in, or by adjusting their relative volumes.
    

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/triple-oscillator#electric-bass)

Electric Bass

Electric bass guitars produce a wide variety of tones. This is just your basic bass with no overdrive or anything like that.

1.  Start with a sine patch and mute OSC 1. Turn OSC 2's volume to 100%.
    
2.  Set "OSC 2 + OSC 3" to PM (Phase Modulation).
    
3.  Turn on the volume envelope and give it a long decay, very short release and an attack around 1/50 or 1/20 of a second.
    
4.  Go to the "FX" page and add a TAP Chorus/Flanger effect. This adds an extra layer of richness to the sound.
    
5.  Now, go back to the "PLUGIN" page and decide where you want OSC 3's volume (that is, the amount of phase modulation between OSC 3 and OSC 2).
    
6.  Finally, reinforce the fundamental (lowest harmonic) of your sound by turning OSC 1 up just a bit. This is optional. Or you can use a triangle wave. Whatever, it's your sound.
    

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/triple-oscillator#kick-drum)

Kick Drum

Ok, this is going to sound weird to some, maybe. Why use TripleOscillator to make a kick drum? The answer: because you can. Also, because it can produce some pretty neat sounds! We're going to use a technique called subtractive synthesis here. To make this tutorial easier to follow, I'm marking with \[tags\] which is the name of the tab you need to be on on the instrument window.

\[Plugin\] To start with, we'll make some noise. Set all the oscillators to produce white noise. The other settings don't matter at this point, as most of them don't have any effect on the noise generation. Then we'll move on to the Env/Lfo tab. That's where the magic happens.

\[Env/Lfo\] Here, we're going to set the volume envelope first. Here are good settings for it:

*   Amt: 1 (full)
    
*   Del: 0
    
*   Att: 0
    
*   Hold: 0
    
*   Dec: 0.248
    
*   Sus: 1
    
*   Rel: 0
    

Ok, try it now. Right now you have a neat little snare drum, kind of a crappy one but still. But read onwards, let's make it into a good kick.

Enable the filter. Select the RC Bandpass (24dB) filter. It is important to pick the right filter, it won't work so well with the 12dB one, unless you wan some weird kick drum... Set the resonance to maximum and cutoff to minimum (that is, 10.0 and 1Hz, respectively). Now, the instrument should sound like a bass guitar that constantly plays the same note. But we're not done yet.

Go to the cutoff envelope, and set these values:

*   Amt: 0.2
    
*   Del: 0.02
    
*   Att: 0
    
*   Hold: 0.05
    
*   Dec: 0.187
    
*   Sus: 1
    
*   Rel: 0
    

Now listen to the sound. It's a kick drum! How awesome is that? Now we can add some character to the sound. Go back to the plugin tab.

\[Plugin\] Now, set the first oscillator to saw wave, set the Crs dial to -24, and try playing different notes (C3 - G4 are good). See how the character of the sound varies slightly! You can vary it further by adjusting the volume of the saw oscillator. Try detuning it with the FL/FR knobs. Turn the SPD knob up to give it some stereo effect. You can also try different waveforms to see how they affect the sound. You can even change the second oscillator, it's enough that there's one noise oscillator to produce enough frequencies to subtract from. You can try all kinds of weird things now to create weird, cool and fun bass/kick drum sounds, and you have much more control and fine-tune over it than you have in Kicker.

# sfxr | 简体中文 (1.3.x) | User manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/sfxr.md)
.

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252F9zGGgIggkMrmuZVKYfzy%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D1392ab02-555d-4c48-82be-c7bad1680904&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d4195eb4&sv=2)

sfxr

sfxr is a port of [the original sfxr tool](http://www.drpetter.se/project_sfxr.html)
. It is used to generate retro video game sounds.

Please note that volume level of the sounds generated by sfxr varies quite a bit, especially with the RAND button.

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/sfxr#generator)

Generator


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The easiest way to use sfxr is with the **generator** tab in the top right corner. In this box are two large text buttons and seven smaller buttons.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/sfxr#text-buttons)

Text Buttons

The RAND button, short for random, generates a completely random sound effect. Because of this, it can generate all kinds of sounds, from cool to boring, and even completely silent ones. To the right of it is the MUTA button, short for mutate. This is similar to randomizing, but instead tweaks your current sound a bit. It's useful to generate variations on a sound, or change a sound slightly to (hopefully) make it better.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/sfxr#icons)

Icons

Underneath the RAND and MUTA buttons are seven icons. These each represent presets that sfxr uses to generate a specific type of sound. In order, they are:

*   ![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252Fmw9g9hEWrfclGzi2kSHI%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9c1bbe65-76d7-43bf-aa15-0d0dd66e91b1&width=35&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b85f68b7&sv=2)\- Coin/Money pickup
    
*   ![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FNsl6vPMqYKhr0ltSK8LA%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dd462928c-c19e-4fb5-9063-9408a70613b0&width=35&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e8b2bcd4&sv=2)\- Shooting
    
*   ![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FkxZhkX7oKaPRQmQrkJPI%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dfd71e898-b893-4df7-a96d-ce350b7dfa68&width=35&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e73366b8&sv=2)\- Explosion
    
*   ![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FP3jTuYlGUoeqmDEQEHpH%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Daaef512a-e469-46d1-8657-588bb3ba4771&width=35&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=319af49f&sv=2)\- Powerup
    
*   ![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FMt9Aw5nkON9taVbYfPeU%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dde9b12c1-74ab-4772-8060-83edbeeecedb&width=35&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=777af328&sv=2)\- Damage
    
*   ![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252F6v2XBSmV3LXX2LazdwXp%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D082ce353-11ac-490a-a73a-4019c62ed001&width=35&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7399bead&sv=2)\- Jump
    
*   ![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252Fgjl9Qd0n9Kpbvb0Q18r5%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D85c569e5-4697-4af6-b34a-52fe9bb7a81d&width=35&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=cf8f38a5&sv=2)\- Interact
    

These generate a wide variety of sounds, while still managing to be recognizable. This is where sfxr really shines.

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/sfxr#controls)

Controls


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

There are also several controls that can be used to tweak a generated sound, or to create on from scratch. To the left of the generator tab are the available waveforms (![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FbaRuy5bzYYGzDSNWH1wu%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3De7594a7f-5699-40f0-9058-d7d2be1df02c&width=136&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=fbfec4d1&sv=2)). Below this are several knobs for changing other parameters.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/sfxr#envelope)

Envelope

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252Fa0ruaeMbmK0J0sfvsE5s%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7d79c4b2-9657-491a-827c-820856eeda62&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2ac4ac58&sv=2)

包络

The top knobs are labeled **Env.**, short for envelope, and are bordered by, and connected with, blue lines. They decide how the volume of the sound changes over time. **ATT**, or attack, determines how long it takes for the sound to reach maximum volume. **HOLD** is how long the sound stays at this level. **SUS**, short for sustain, is how loud the sound gets at the start. Finally, **DEC**, or decay, is how long it takes for the sound to fade out.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/sfxr#frequency)

Frequency

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FKtvQZb5VMUzWB1hiaMq8%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D5bf5b190-e0af-4006-a638-533a3644f2d8&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=fdd5aa02&sv=2)

频率

Underneath the envelope knobs are the green **Freq.** knobs, which control the frequency of the sound. **START** is the start frequency, and **MIN** is the minimum frequency. **SLIDE** makes the sound change frequency over time, with a positive number making it go up, and a negative number making it go down. **D.SLI.**, or Delta slide, affects how fast the frequency changes. Values closer to 1 or -1 make the frequency change faster, while values closer to 0 make it slower. A negative value reverses the slide, making an upwards slide go downwards and vice versa. **V.DEP.**, or vibrato depth, changes how much of an effect vibrato has, while **V.SPD.**, or vibrato speed, changes how fast the vibrato is.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/sfxr#change)

Change

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252F1hm7qI0vRZfxpFw9wsuo%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dbde5ceb4-15bb-49ac-8dab-c2fb7f401989&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=8faf8b88&sv=2)

变化

In the middle and on the right of the knobs are two red knobs, labelled **Change**. **C.AMT** stands for change amount, when you have positive values, the note will at a point, decided by the **C.SPD** - change speed or suddenly jump to a higher frequency. If you set the knob to negative values the new frequency will be lower than the original. This new frequency will last for as long as the note is held. Change amount set to 0 doesn't affect the sound. Change amount set to 0.25 raises the tone with one semitone. Change amount set to 1 raises the tone with 3 octaves and 4 semitones. The Change speed takes the note frequency in consideration on it's calculations, a low frequency note plays the original note longer before jumping to the new frequency than a high note does. Even if change speed is set to 0, the note will jump.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/sfxr#square)

Square

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FnSshn5sfuMBREgQgjBjb%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D3efa691d-0b23-4c2d-bd9c-a9fa9a118a44&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2100017f&sv=2)

方波

In the middle and on the left of the knobs are two orange knobs with options for when a square wave is used, labelled **Square**. **S.Duty** changes the duty cycle, shifting the lowest parts of the square wave up, while **S.SPD** changes the duty cycle over time in a sweep.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/sfxr#repeat)

Repeat

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FwBR64Wa20KKyTyvEP835%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7a73a064-8a00-40fd-a90b-61c9654f5d41&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=141874a4&sv=2)

重复

Under the middle and on the left of the knobs is a section called **Repeat**, with only one grey knob, **R.SPD.** or repeat speed. Setting this knob to anything other than zero will play the sound twice, with a slight delay in between. The higher the value, the shorter the delay.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/sfxr#phaser)

Phaser

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252F8rWyaU70kHpl3QMkPHtm%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dd1634b11-d18f-4441-bd98-f9711f05da68&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c834daba&sv=2)

相位器

Under the middle and to the right is a section labelled **Phaser**, with two silver knobs. **PH.OFF.** or phaser offset, and **PH.S.** or phaser sweep. A phaser combines a signal with an offset version of itself, leading to phase cancellation in some frequencies. In practical terms this leads to a shifting filter effect.

### 

[](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/jian-ti-zhong-wen/yue-qi-cha-jian/sfxr#filter)

Filter

![](https://docs.lmms.io/user-manual/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3710990805-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FZRacsG2wvxpXwri0XH6G%252Fuploads%252FAt2Gfr0bYVNK43G8bcWf%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D5de28228-7d40-4288-9829-6a690ed04a99&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=828c290d&sv=2)

滤波器

All the way at the bottom, there are five purple knobs, labelled **Filter**. The ones that start with L are low pass filter settings, and the ones that start with H are high pass filter settings. **LF.C.** and **HF.C.** are low pass cutoff and high pass cutoff, respectively. On a low pass filter, frequencies below the cutoff are dampened. **LF.S.** and **HF.S.** are low pass sweep and high pass sweep. Positive values increase the filter cutoff with time (lowering it for low pass and raising it for high pass), and negative values decrease it, with numbers further from zero changing the cutoff more. Finally, the low pass filter has an additional resonance knob, **LF.R.**, to increase or decrease resonance. With resonance, frequencies near the cutoff point are boosted.
